- **kv-bench**: Benchmark the kv-cache stack at three levels — raw NIXL plugin, the L2 block tier, and the live engine through the proxy with llama-benchy. Enforces the preconditions and the interpretation traps that make a sweep meaningful. Also trigger with /kv-bench. ([pmallapp-amd/llm-stacks](https://github.com/pmallapp-amd/llm-stacks/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/kv-bench) · ★1)
- **kv-cache**: Designs prefix-aware KV cache and radix-tree caching for LLM inference engines. Use when implementing or refactoring block tables, paged KV, radix prefix cache, cache hit/miss, eviction, or when the user mentions nano-vllm BlockManager, nano-sglang/mini-sglang RadixCache, or Agent-generated inference frameworks. ([HuangPuStar/MetaInfer](https://github.com/HuangPuStar/MetaInfer/tree/HEAD/metainfer/tasks/gen_infer_framework/notebooks/01_framework_design/kv_cache) · ★139)
- **kv-cache-compression**: Enables 8x KV cache compression with minimal training overhead to improve reasoning accuracy by allowing more token generation within computational budgets. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/kv-cache-compression) · ★7)
- **kv-cache-enabled-generation-profiling**: Use when you need to measure the wall-clock time overhead that constrained generation (e.g., a prefix-tree index restricting valid token sequences) adds over normal LLM decoding under identical hardware and settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/kv-cache-enabled-generation-profiling))
- **kv-cache-eviction-eval**: Evaluates the quality of learned key-value (KV) cache eviction policies in preserving long-context reasoning and generation capabilities under strict memory constraints. It measures how well different compression strategies retain critical tokens without access to query-specific attention scores during the compression phase.
